## LOC - Localisation / coordinate head (V1284 coordhead, float output, lowrefine, head retrain)

Impact: Only in-window lever with a public proof: +0.002 on our base (probe 31), +0.007 inside x138 (CONTEXT.md l.1495-1504). lowrefine scored neutral (d1 = d2 = 0) and stays in both finals. Round 8: a constant +0.1 vox z shift gains +0.0016 in-sample on the x138 lab but ~+0.0003 transfer-adjusted -> post-deadline (I-029); post-deadline also axial localisation of z-elongated 6bba nuclei (I-261).

<a id="div"></a>
## DIV - Divisions (safe-division gates, DivNet, DaughterNet insertion, forkmid/forkblob, DeepCenter threshold)

Impact: 0.1 x div_J; public div_J <= 0.36 (probe 26); realised +0.001 twice (probes 28, 35), insertion is inside K2. The fork-REMOVAL family is exhausted on exp60 (forkmid, forkblob, ANTIPAR FAIL; DNVETO dropped; K3b FAIL): lab break-even FP:TP >= 7.7 at J 0.129 (CONTEXT.md l.1918-1937; research/wf_ideas_v4/round2/report.json new_facts (7)). Round 3: the insertion is not a dense-clip risk (private harm needs OOF evaluable precision < ~11%) and the whole x138 tail is saturated (research/wf_ideas_v4/round3/report.json new_facts (11), (13)). Round 9 anatomy (exp60 x138 lab): the division loss is missing forks (31/83 FN with no fork within +-4 frames) plus isolated false forks (15/25 FP), not timing/position offsets; GT-noise mimicry reach 1/83 (research/wf_ideas_v4/round9/scan_recheck.json R9-NF-division-fn-anatomy-x138, R9-RC-A).

## NODES - Node budget & recall (track length, threshold, pruning, readmit/gapfill, hole fill, leaf prune)
